5.7.1	F&B & Guest Management	Accounting	The system should support entry and maintenance of data in accounts related information tables. The primary usage of this data will be to derive accounts for billing records and interface with the Finance system.
5.7.2	F&B & Guest Management	Accounting	The system should be able to store account information for each Offset/Balancing Account by Payment Type (e.g. cash, bank card, tax, etc.). At minimum, the below fields should be included for storage:  / - Company I Attraction | Sales office | F&B or Retail Product or Service. / - GL Account. / - Profit Center. / - Cost Center.
5.7.3	F&B & Guest Management	Accounting	The system should be able to store account information for each Revenue Account by Item or Item Type (Merchandise, etc.). At minimum, the below fields should be included for storage: / - Company I Attraction | Sales office | F&B or Retail Product or Service. / - GL Account. / - Profit Center. / - Cost Center.
5.7.4	F&B & Guest Management	Accounting	The system should accept entry of ERP /Business software chart of account structure. Account coding will require company code, GL account, WBS (Work Breakdown Structure) code, and cost object (cost center, internal order, Attractions element) or profit center. Only one of the cost objects or profit center will be entered for each transaction.
5.7.5	F&B & Guest Management	Accounting	The system should validate that required A/R account fields are populated with correct length and format based on the requirements provided by ERP.
5.7.6	F&B & Guest Management	Accounting	The system allow general ledger revenue rules to be configured to trigger account allocation based on product sale and redemption events. Revenue allocation rules can be defined for all products and sales channels.
5.7.7	F&B & Guest Management	Accounting	The system should be able to sell multi-attraction and combination tickets with configurable revenue split. Revenue rules should apply financial clearings based on products and client behaviors. The system should support revenue split by: / - Sale / - Sale location / - Sale channel / - Operating area / - Usage / - First usage / - Redemption / - Expiry / - Promotion usage
5.7.8	F&B & Guest Management	Accounting	The system should be able to use of a unique Order or Reference number (PNR) for each transaction, which can be communicated to the Payment Gateway, Acquiring Bank and the ERP system for reconciliation purposes.
5.7.9	F&B & Guest Management	Accounting	The system should provide full visibility of all codes as well as an easy and automated way to reconcile credit card payments (VPOS, POS) versus ticketing payments.
5.7.10	F&B & Guest Management	Accounting	The system should create standard debit and credit journal entries when an admission item is paid for (e.g., debit cash, credit unearned revenue), and additional journal entries when a ticket is used/scanned (e.g., debit unearned revenue, credit revenue). These entries will be created for all payments types (cash, credit, internal, 3rd party) and also applicable to admissions sold online.
5.7.11	F&B & Guest Management	Accounting	The system should be able to create standard debit and credit journal entries when a non-admission item (retail products) is paid for (e.g. debit cash, credit revenue).
5.7.12	F&B & Guest Management	Accounting	The system should be able generate offsetting entries when an admission ticket expires (e.g., debit unearned revenue, credit revenue). If a ticket is not used, the system will need to get revenue from unearned to earn. A ticket will be considered expired after the date/time has passed
5.7.13	F&B & Guest Management	Accounting	The system should be able to create journal entries for discount records from an order (if applicable). The order will indicate whether a discount has been given. Discount journal entry records will be created in addition to the standard journal entry records.
5.7.14	F&B & Guest Management	Accounting	The system should be able to create journal entries for refund transactions. These journal entries should correctly handle associated tax liabilities.
5.7.15	F&B & Guest Management	Accounting	The system should be able to create journal entries for loyalty points redemption. These journal entries should correctly handle associated tax liabilities.
5.7.16	F&B & Guest Management	Accounting	The system should be able to validate that all transactions are balanced. Total debits must equal total credits per transaction.
5.7.17	F&B & Guest Management	Accounting	The system should realize digital wallet revenues upon redemption not initial top up. Upon purchase all revenues from the digital wallet purchase should be allocated to a deferred revenue account based on the point of purchase.  / Upon redemption, digital wallet funds should be transferred to the appropriate ledger account.
5.7.18	F&B & Guest Management	Accounting	The system should be able to recognize when an external system (E.g. Partner Vendor) has been used to make a charge against the digital wallet and to allocate revenues to a specific ledger account. The system should correctly handle tax liabilities associated with such transactions.
5.7.19	F&B & Guest Management	Accounting	The system should be able to recognize the below transactions for correct accounting treatment: / - Free tickets or sales / - Unused/Expired tickets or wallet credits
5.7.20	F&B & Guest Management	Accounting	The system should support real-time or near real-time processing of journal entries and revenue allocation as configured in the system.
5.7.21	F&B & Guest Management	Accounting	The system should be able to push cash, credit card and voucher payments to the Bulk Reconciliation system.
5.7.22	F&B & Guest Management	Accounting	The system should follow revenue recognition rules according to IFRS (International Financial Reporting Standards).
5.7.23	F&B & Guest Management	Accounting	An API must deliver the sales transactions to the FE accounting system which will be in charge of the invoicing.

5.7.87	F&B & Guest Management	Accounting	The system shall support configurable mapping between operational transactions and accounting accounts. Examples include Ticket Revenue to Revenue Account, Wallet Liability to Deferred Revenue, VAT to Tax Liability, Gift Cards to Liability Accounts, and Commissions to Expense Accounts. Mapping rules shall be configurable by product, channel, location, and transaction type.
5.7.88	F&B & Guest Management	Accounting	The system shall support multiple legal entities, companies, business units, and attractions while maintaining separate accounting books, reporting structures, and financial controls. Shared products and services shall support entity-level allocation rules.
5.7.89	F&B & Guest Management	Accounting	The system shall support fiscal year management, fiscal periods, month-end and year-end closing. Authorized users shall be able to open, close, lock, unlock, and re-open accounting periods with complete audit tracking.

5.7.92	F&B & Guest Management	Accounting	The system shall support chargeback notification handling, dispute management, evidence collection, investigation workflows, resolution tracking, and financial adjustments. Track chargeback status, amount, payment method, gateway, and outcome.

5.7.95	F&B & Guest Management	Accounting	The system shall support configurable booking fees, service fees, payment processing fees, convenience fees, delivery fees, and channel-specific fees. Fees may be configured by product, channel, customer type, partner, and promotion.
5.7.96	F&B & Guest Management	Accounting	The system shall support subscription creation, renewal, upgrade, downgrade, suspension, cancellation, expiry, and reactivation. Applicable to memberships, annual passes, recurring services, and subscription-based products.
5.7.97	F&B & Guest Management	Accounting	The system shall support gift card liability accounting, redemption accounting, expiry accounting, breakage revenue recognition, transfers, refunds, and financial reporting. Track balances and lifecycle events.
5.7.98	F&B & Guest Management	Accounting	The system shall support fixed, percentage-based, tiered, partner, reseller, affiliate, and agent commissions. Support commission calculation, accruals, settlements, reversals, and reporting.
5.7.99	F&B & Guest Management	Accounting	The system shall provide full financial audit support including journal traceability, transaction traceability, change history, reconciliation history, approval history, and audit reporting. All financial actions shall be fully auditable.
5.7.100	F&B & Guest Management	Accounting	The system shall support PCI-compliant integration architecture and secure payment processing. Sensitive cardholder information shall not be stored unless specifically certified and authorized. Support tokenization and secure payment integrations.
5.8.1	F&B & Guest Management	Cash Handling	The system should allow allocation of a deposit box for each cashier.
5.8.2	F&B & Guest Management	Cash Handling	The system should only allow one session per user.
5.8.3	F&B & Guest Management	Cash Handling	The system should allow the cashier to enter a total amount counted, or to count by denomination. For denomination counts, the cashier counts and enters each denomination separately and each count is saved into the system.
5.8.4	F&B & Guest Management	Cash Handling	The system should allow modification of initial fund of a cashier till based on user rights.
5.8.5	F&B & Guest Management	Cash Handling	The system should allow print and reprint of deposit box transactions. The output should be bilingual (Arabic and English) and simplified for tax handling.
5.8.6	F&B & Guest Management	Cash Handling	The system should allow automated close out of one or more deposit boxes.
5.8.7	F&B & Guest Management	Cash Handling	The system should automatically link the amount on a payment card reader with the order amount on the POS without requiring any user input.
5.8.8	F&B & Guest Management	Cash Handling	The system should allow the supervisor to withdraw some cash during the day from an cashier’s cash float and trace it in the system.
5.8.9	F&B & Guest Management	Cash Handling	The system should make sure the controlling team gets notifications regarding the sessions opened/closed and even if one transaction is cancelled by cashiers.
5.9.1	F&B & Guest Management	Shift opening and closing	The system should be able to manage end of day shift closing and provide the ability to close out each cash register.
5.9.2	F&B & Guest Management	Shift opening and closing	The system should have the ability to do a "blind" cashier close-out.  Over-shorts should be captured and recorded.
5.9.3	F&B & Guest Management	Shift opening and closing	The system should be able to generate an end of shift report for the cashiers. Report should contain consolidated data (amounts collected by means of payment, quantity issued by product type, amounts collected by product type, number of products void by product type).
5.9.4	F&B & Guest Management	Shift opening and closing	The system should be able to provide a detailed log of transactions for each till. Detailed log of transaction should be always accessible, searchable and printable at back office.
5.9.5	F&B & Guest Management	Shift opening and closing	The system should allow automatic closing of a cashier's session after a configurable time period. The supervisor should be notified on closing of the session.
5.9.6	F&B & Guest Management	Shift opening and closing	The system must provide an alert when the expected value of the till and the current value are not the same at the end of the shift.
5.9.7	F&B & Guest Management	Shift opening and closing	The system should be able to generate a shift report with full transaction history. The Shift Report is a summary of sales from the most recent till opening until the most recent till closing.
5.9.8	F&B & Guest Management	Shift opening and closing	The system should allow the option of configuring the requirement of a supervisor's approval for opening and closing a cashier's session. For example, to open a cashier’s session, the cashier details the cash denomination in the till and the supervisor declares the cash float amount.
5.9.9	F&B & Guest Management	Shift opening and closing	The system should support  generation of a temporary report at the closing of a session  with total amounts per payment type, including cash denomination. Possibility to write comments on the report.
5.9.10	F&B & Guest Management	Shift opening and closing	The system should automatically generate consolidated payment reports each day and send to the desired teams.
5.10.1	F&B & Guest Management	Receipt	The system should provide a simple way to print receipts for guests depending on their purchases and their consumptions (i.e. pay‐per‐use).
5.10.2	F&B & Guest Management	Receipt	The system should have a receipt module that supports receipt layout customization and guest tracking.
5.10.3	F&B & Guest Management	Receipt	The system should show all required fields on the receipt in accordance with VAT regulations.
5.11.1	F&B & Guest Management	Double-Entry Financial Ledger	Provides accounting-grade financial recording of all platform transactions.
5.11.2	F&B & Guest Management	Double-Entry Financial Ledger	Double Entry Accounting / Each financial transaction shall create balanced debit and credit entries.
5.11.3	F&B & Guest Management	Double-Entry Financial Ledger	Immutable Financial Records / Posted transactions shall not be deleted or altered.
5.11.4	F&B & Guest Management	Double-Entry Financial Ledger	Journal Management / System shall support accounting journals and transaction classifications.
5.11.5	F&B & Guest Management	Double-Entry Financial Ledger	Financial Audit Reporting / System shall provide traceability between operational transactions and accounting records.
5.11.6	F&B & Guest Management	Double-Entry Financial Ledger	Multi-Currency Support / System shall support accounting entries across multiple currencies.
5.12.1	F&B & Guest Management	Revenue Recognition	Supports recognition of revenue according to accounting policies rather than immediately upon sale.
5.12.2	F&B & Guest Management	Revenue Recognition	Deferred Revenue / System shall support deferred revenue accounting.
5.12.3	F&B & Guest Management	Revenue Recognition	Recognition Rules / Revenue recognition schedules shall be configurable by product type.

6.1.1	Retail POS	Wallet	The system should share all transactions and receipts generated in the system with the external e-invoicing solution.
6.1.2	Retail POS	Wallet	The system should allow printing of a ticket receipt if the guests requests for it. It should also be shareable with the guest by email.
6.1.3	Retail POS	Wallet	The system should have the option to configure the issuing of a payment receipt depending on the price level (For example: free ticket, no payment receipt)
6.1.4	Retail POS	Wallet	The system should have the possibility to send receipts, order confirmations and other management documents by email, in PDF format.
6.1.5	Retail POS	Wallet	The system should be able to Generate reports with admission types/information.
6.1.6	Retail POS	Wallet	The system should be able to use and allow standardized reporting structure/software .
6.1.7	Retail POS	Wallet	The system should be able to report supporting nightly balancing: / 1.Tender balancing with export in batch file. / 2.Handling consecutive days of open transactions / exceptions report.
6.1.8	Retail POS	Wallet	The system should have the ability to retrieve information "on the fly" for items, (e.g., keyword, item #, description, category etc.) in user-friendly format such as pull-down menus and/or auto fill fields.
6.1.9	Retail POS	Wallet	The system should be able to report historical sales look up by item, ticket number etc..
6.1.10	Retail POS	Wallet	The system should be able to report on daily transactions summarized by:  / 1.Currency amount sold. / 2.Currency amount discounted. / 3.Tender type. / 4.Units sold.
6.1.11	Retail POS	Wallet	The system should be able to report on specific transactions by:  / 1.Teller/employees. / 2.Price overrides. / 3.Refunds.
6.1.12	Retail POS	Wallet	The system should be able to search on any transaction in the system, filtering by date range, sales station/workstation, time range, cashier, client name, email, amount, transaction status result, order ID / PNR or channel. The system should have a configurable value to define the maximum range of days that can be searched.

6.1.14	Retail POS	Wallet	The system should have scheduling of report generation and delivery to web address location or list of email addresses.

6.1.17	Retail POS	Wallet	The system should be able to report on discount types with POS ID, Staff info, date and time, ticket type, attraction type, location, sales channel etc.
6.1.18	Retail POS	Wallet	The system should be able to ability to run sales history reports based on multiple factors, including guest, item, date, category, teller.

Upgrade split: / No retroactive adjustment will be made, previously settled income remains unchanged. / The unsettled income (original sale price + upgrade price difference - previously settled income) will be reallocated based on the updated revenue sharing rules.

10.1.1	Games & F&B Integration	Kitchen Display & Inventory Sync	The system should allow send special request comments/directions on the kitchen display/printers for the kitchen preparation guest requests/inputs.
10.1.2	Games & F&B Integration	Kitchen Display & Inventory Sync	The system should be able to sync all the recipes from the inventory management on real-time or timed intervals, to be able to display products and menu buttons.
10.1.3	Games & F&B Integration	Kitchen Display & Inventory Sync	The system should be able to sync all the recipe orders to manage and reflect on inventory and order management reports and further use this data for re-order levels.
10.1.4	Games & F&B Integration	Kitchen Display & Inventory Sync	The system should be able to sync all the SKU products and Standalone SKU products  from the inventory management and make it available to sell as per availability and pricing strategy.
10.1.5	Games & F&B Integration	Kitchen Display & Inventory Sync	The system should have the ability to record the local /in store inventory( for in store stock view/management).
10.2.1	Games & F&B Integration	Game & Ride Payment & Redemption	The system should allow the usage of digital wallet credits for pay as you go for Redemption /Skill games /Rides /Video games
10.2.2	Games & F&B Integration	Game & Ride Payment & Redemption	The system should allow accumulation of redemption tickets credits into guest's digital wallet through multiple ways: / - Ticket-based system: Integration with Ticket-eater machines to add the credits for the physical inserted redemption tickets / - Ticketless system: Integration with Redemption games to digitally load the credits for the redemption tickets after a guest has completed a game.
10.2.3	Games & F&B Integration	Game & Ride Payment & Redemption	The system should provide the ability to view all the credits stored in a digital wallet at multiple kiosks; operator kiosks and self-service kiosks.
10.2.4	Games & F&B Integration	Game & Ride Payment & Redemption	The system should allow usage of redemption tickets credits in the digital wallet at redemption counter for prizes. Prizes on redemption counter, skill games, direct pay machines (e.g. cranes) should be integrated with the inventory system.
10.2.5	Games & F&B Integration	Game & Ride Payment & Redemption	The system should have the ability to add credits for free games and rides to the  digital wallet of a guest which can be used across applicable attractions.
10.2.6	Games & F&B Integration	Game & Ride Payment & Redemption	The system should have ability to define multiple types of readers (minimum three).  The properties of the readers should be configurable: / - type of credit accepted at a reader / - amount of credit required for a reader / - attraction type linked to the reader / Through these properties, multiple combinations of readers can be created to support the business requirements. For example: one reader for Video games , one reader for Skill games and one reader for rides.
10.2.7	Games & F&B Integration	Game & Ride Payment & Redemption	Gameplay validation / Prices to be defined for each game / Assign prices for the Group. / Game Reader  / - Access to the particular game / - Bonus deduction should be done first / Package / - To create a package with specific games / - To define the validity for the entitlement for the games
10.2.8	Games & F&B Integration	Game & Ride Payment & Redemption	Ticket to play all games / rides in the amusement park
10.2.11	Games & F&B Integration	Game & Ride Payment & Redemption	Ticket to play free specific games / rides in the amusement park Unlimited times
10.2.12	Games & F&B Integration	Game & Ride Payment & Redemption	Ticket to play free specific games / rides in the amusement park limited times
10.2.13	Games & F&B Integration	Game & Ride Payment & Redemption	Realtime validation of Credit/card balance, bonus and entitlements before game starts / - Customers tap the card before playing the game and amount should be deducted based on the available balance and show the remaining balance / - Customer taps the card, and it should be allowed to play in case if the customer allowed to play specific game
10.2.14	Games & F&B Integration	Game & Ride Payment & Redemption	Free games with color glow reader / -To show free games with a different color on the card reader, so that customer can understand all these games are free
10.2.15	Games & F&B Integration	Game & Ride Payment & Redemption	Dynamic pricing for games / - System should provide an ability to set up peak / nonpeak pricing for all the games / rides based on specific date / time
10.2.16	Games & F&B Integration	Game & Ride Payment & Redemption	Retry variable price / -This is applicable to skilled games where the customer plays a game, and before the game finishes, a pop-up appears with the question, ‘Do you want to continue?’ By Tapping the RFID card, it should deduct a lower price than the original amount, as this is a retry transaction
10.2.17	Games & F&B Integration	Game & Ride Payment & Redemption	Normal & VIP price / - To have one reader to show both Normal Price & VIP Price for specific game. / - For VIP, customer buys VIP product. All the rides / games have specific prices assigned for Normal & VIP.
10.2.18	Games & F&B Integration	Game & Ride Payment & Redemption	Retap delay (There should be a delay configured between the first tap to the second tap and so on) / - Customers tap the card to play a game. Customer retries immediately and it will deduct the money again. To avoid this scenario, there should be a delay to Retap after few seconds (5 seconds) and then customer will be able to tap the card
10.2.19	Games & F&B Integration	Game & Ride Payment & Redemption	Custom theme for Readers / - Skilled Games should have one theme for the reader; Rides should have another theme. To be able to control from BOS.
10.2.20	Games & F&B Integration	Game & Ride Payment & Redemption	Check balance / - There should be a reader to check the balance for the customer
10.2.21	Games & F&B Integration	Game & Ride Payment & Redemption	Card expiry based on last recharge & last activity / - Any card issued should have expiry of 6 months based on the last card activity date or last recharge
10.2.22	Games & F&B Integration	Game & Ride Payment & Redemption	Card Top + Bonus / - A customer can Top up of 100 AED should include (100 AED + 25 AED (Bonus)) / - This Bonus allowed to play only Games / Rides (not allowed to redeem in F&B / Retail) Should be configurable / - Bonus to be used only in Amusement park rides / games / - No Refund allowed on the remaining balance / - Bonus cannot be refunded. / - There should be validity set for the Bonus. / - Option to provide additional Bonus to the customer on demand

13.3.1	Developer & API Management	API Business Coverage	All business functionality available through the TAIS user interface shall be exposed through documented APIs unless restricted by security, compliance or operational considerations.
13.3.2	Developer & API Management	API Business Coverage	APIs shall support ticket creation, modification, cancellation, exchange, upgrade, validation, inventory, availability and pricing operations.
13.3.3	Developer & API Management	API Business Coverage	APIs shall support membership creation, renewal, upgrade, downgrade, freeze, entitlement validation and membership status retrieval.
13.3.4	Developer & API Management	API Business Coverage	APIs shall support point accrual, point redemption, balance inquiry, loyalty tier management and rewards retrieval.
13.3.5	Developer & API Management	API Business Coverage	APIs shall support wallet funding, spending, refunds, balance inquiry, transaction history and wallet status management.
13.3.6	Developer & API Management	API Business Coverage	APIs shall support access validation, entry events, exit events, occupancy management and access entitlement verification.
13.3.7	Developer & API Management	API Business Coverage	APIs shall support resource availability, reservations, allocation, scheduling and capacity management.
13.3.8	Developer & API Management	API Business Coverage	APIs shall support guest profile creation, updates, segmentation, communication preferences and activity history retrieval.
13.3.9	Developer & API Management	API Business Coverage	APIs shall support menu retrieval, order creation, order status, kitchen status, inventory updates and promotions.
13.3.10	Developer & API Management	API Business Coverage	APIs shall support product catalogs, inventory availability, promotions, orders, exchanges and returns.
13.3.11	Developer & API Management	API Business Coverage	APIs shall support reservation creation, modification, cancellation, waitlists and availability checks.
13.3.12	Developer & API Management	API Business Coverage	APIs shall expose pricing calculations, price recommendations and pricing rule management.
13.3.13	Developer & API Management	API Business Coverage	APIs shall expose AI services including forecasting, recommendations, dynamic pricing, fraud detection and AI assistant functions.
13.3.14	Developer & API Management	API Business Coverage	APIs shall expose operational, financial, attendance, membership and sales reporting data.
13.3.15	Developer & API Management	API Business Coverage	APIs shall support email, SMS, push notifications, WhatsApp notifications and notification status retrieval.
13.3.16	Developer & API Management	API Business Coverage	APIs shall support installation, activation and management of third-party integrations and marketplace extensions.
13.3.17	Developer & API Management	API Business Coverage	System shall publish business events such as ticket sales, ticket validation, membership changes, loyalty transactions, wallet transactions, reservations, access events, refunds, upgrades, and operational activities for consumption by internal and external systems.
13.3.18	Developer & API Management	API Business Coverage	System shall allow authorized internal and external systems to subscribe to business events based on configurable subscription rules and permissions.
13.3.19	Developer & API Management	API Business Coverage	System shall support replay of historical events for recovery, synchronization, troubleshooting, and integration reprocessing purposes.
13.3.20	Developer & API Management	API Business Coverage	System shall support configurable retention policies for published events including archival, expiration, and compliance-based retention requirements.
13.3.21	Developer & API Management	API Business Coverage	System shall maintain complete audit history of published events, subscriptions, deliveries, retries, failures, acknowledgements, and processing outcomes.
13.3.22	Developer & API Management	API Business Coverage	System shall support filtering, routing, and delivery of events based on event type, tenant, venue, business unit, source system, or configurable business rules.
13.3.23	Developer & API Management	API Business Coverage	System shall monitor event delivery performance, processing status, failed deliveries, retry attempts, latency metrics, and subscriber health.
13.3.24	Developer & API Management	API Business Coverage	To provide the ability to enable license for the API only for the specific module. Example. APIs are exposed only for ticketing excluding resource management, Seating Module, etc

22.1.1	Marketing & CRM	Campaign Management	Campaign Creation || System shall allow authorized users to create marketing campaigns across email, SMS, WhatsApp, push notifications, mobile app, website banners, social media integrations, and third-party communication channels. Campaigns shall support draft, approval, scheduling, and publishing workflows.
22.1.2	Marketing & CRM	Campaign Management	Campaign Templates || System shall provide reusable campaign templates for ticket sales, memberships, annual passes, loyalty promotions, events, seasonal promotions, abandoned carts, birthday campaigns, and retention campaigns. Templates shall be configurable and reusable across multiple venues and brands.
22.1.3	Marketing & CRM	Campaign Management	Multi-Channel Campaign Execution || A single campaign shall be deployable simultaneously across multiple channels including email, SMS, WhatsApp, push notifications, mobile app notifications, website banners, and external integrations.
22.1.4	Marketing & CRM	Campaign Management	Campaign Scheduling || System shall support immediate, scheduled, recurring, event-triggered, and timezone-aware campaign execution with configurable send windows and blackout periods.
22.1.5	Marketing & CRM	Campaign Management	Campaign Approval Workflow || System shall support configurable approval workflows before campaign activation, including creator, reviewer, approver, and publisher roles with full audit tracking.
22.1.6	Marketing & CRM	Campaign Management	Audience Selection || System shall allow campaigns to target guests based on CRM segments, loyalty status, memberships, purchase history, visit history, demographics, behavioral activity, wallet activity, survey responses, and custom rules.
22.1.7	Marketing & CRM	Campaign Management	Dynamic Personalization || Campaign content shall support dynamic personalization using guest attributes such as name, language, loyalty tier, membership type, favorite attractions, previous purchases, and visit history.
22.1.8	Marketing & CRM	Campaign Management	Campaign Budget Management || System shall allow definition of campaign budgets, expected revenue, expected conversion rates, and track actual performance against targets.
22.1.9	Marketing & CRM	Campaign Management	Campaign Asset Library || System shall provide centralized storage and management of campaign assets including images, videos, PDFs, templates, banners, QR codes, and promotional materials.
22.1.10	Marketing & CRM	Campaign Management	Campaign Performance Dashboard || System shall provide dashboards showing sends, deliveries, opens, clicks, conversions, ticket sales, membership sales, loyalty engagement, wallet top-ups, revenue generated, ROI, and campaign profitability.
22.1.11	Marketing & CRM	Campaign Management	Promotion Integration || Campaigns shall integrate with the promotion engine and allow automatic application of discounts, vouchers, promo codes, loyalty rewards, bundles, and special offers.
22.1.12	Marketing & CRM	Campaign Management	Ticketing Campaigns || System shall support campaigns specifically for ticket sales, events, attractions, seat inventory promotions, capacity-driven offers, and last-minute sales.
22.1.13	Marketing & CRM	Campaign Management	Membership Campaigns || System shall support campaigns for membership acquisition, renewals, upgrades, expiration reminders, and membership benefit awareness.
22.1.14	Marketing & CRM	Campaign Management	Loyalty Campaigns || System shall support loyalty-focused campaigns including points multipliers, tier upgrades, reward redemptions, referral programs, and retention initiatives.
22.1.15	Marketing & CRM	Campaign Management	Event-Triggered Campaigns || Campaigns shall automatically trigger based on predefined events such as ticket purchase, membership expiration, birthday, anniversary, loyalty milestone, wallet top-up, abandoned cart, or first visit.
22.1.16	Marketing & CRM	Campaign Management	AI Campaign Recommendations || AI shall recommend campaign audiences, offers, communication channels, send times, frequency, and content based on historical campaign performance and guest behavior.
22.1.17	Marketing & CRM	Campaign Management	AI Subject Line Optimization || AI shall generate and test subject lines, notification titles, and campaign messaging to improve open rates and engagement.
22.1.18	Marketing & CRM	Campaign Management	A/B Testing || System shall support A/B and multivariate testing of campaign content, offers, channels, send times, subject lines, and creative assets.
22.1.19	Marketing & CRM	Campaign Management	Campaign Attribution || System shall track which campaign influenced ticket sales, membership purchases, loyalty activity, wallet usage, reservations, and revenue generation.
22.1.20	Marketing & CRM	Campaign Management	Campaign Audit Trail || System shall maintain a complete audit trail of campaign creation, edits, approvals, executions, results, and user actions for compliance and reporting purposes.

22.2.13	Marketing & CRM	Guest Profiles & Data Management	Loyalty History || Guest profiles shall display loyalty points earned, redeemed, adjusted, expired, rewards claimed, tier progression, and loyalty transactions.
22.2.14	Marketing & CRM	Guest Profiles & Data Management	Wallet History || Guest profiles shall display wallet balances, top-ups, spending activity, refunds, transfers, vouchers, gift cards, and wallet transaction history.

22.2.17	Marketing & CRM	Guest Profiles & Data Management	Case & Support History || Guest profiles shall display complaints, support tickets, service requests, incidents, refunds, escalations, and resolution history.
22.2.18	Marketing & CRM	Guest Profiles & Data Management	Consent Management || Guest profiles shall store marketing consents, privacy preferences, opt-ins, opt-outs, waiver acknowledgements, and consent audit trails.
22.2.19	Marketing & CRM	Guest Profiles & Data Management	Document Management || System shall store guest-related documents including waivers, liability forms, ID documents, contracts, uploaded files, and signed agreements.
22.2.20	Marketing & CRM	Guest Profiles & Data Management	Guest Lifetime Value (LTV) || System shall automatically calculate and display guest lifetime value based on historical revenue, visits, memberships, loyalty engagement, and future value predictions.
22.2.21	Marketing & CRM	Guest Profiles & Data Management	Guest Engagement Score || System shall calculate engagement scores based on visits, purchases, campaigns, app usage, loyalty participation, and interactions.
22.2.22	Marketing & CRM	Guest Profiles & Data Management	AI Guest Insights || AI shall analyze guest behavior and provide insights, trends, interests, risks, opportunities, and recommendations for engagement.
22.2.23	Marketing & CRM	Guest Profiles & Data Management	AI Churn Prediction || AI shall identify guests at risk of disengagement, membership cancellation, reduced spending, or inactivity and provide retention recommendations.
22.2.24	Marketing & CRM	Guest Profiles & Data Management	AI Next Best Action || AI shall recommend the next best action, offer, campaign, membership, reward, event, or product for each guest.
22.2.25	Marketing & CRM	Guest Profiles & Data Management	AI Audience Classification || AI shall automatically classify guests into marketing audiences such as VIP, family, tourist, frequent visitor, inactive, high spender, or membership prospect.
22.2.26	Marketing & CRM	Guest Profiles & Data Management	Customer 360 Dashboard || System shall provide a unified dashboard displaying guest KPIs, profile summary, financial value, loyalty status, membership status, wallet balance, and engagement metrics.
22.2.27	Marketing & CRM	Guest Profiles & Data Management	CRM APIs || All guest profile data, preferences, transactions, relationships, and activities shall be available through secured APIs for integration with third-party CRM, ERP, CDP, and marketing platforms.
22.2.28	Marketing & CRM	Guest Profiles & Data Management	CRM Audit Trail || System shall maintain a complete audit trail of profile creation, updates, merges, data access, consent changes, and administrative actions for compliance and governance purposes.
22.3.1	Marketing & CRM	Case Management & SLA	Case Creation || System shall support creation of customer service cases originating from website, mobile app, POS, kiosk, call center, CRM, email, chat, WhatsApp, API integrations, and internal users.
22.3.2	Marketing & CRM	Case Management & SLA	Case Classification || System shall support configurable case categories, subcategories, priorities, severity levels, service types, business units, venues, attractions, and departments.
22.3.3	Marketing & CRM	Case Management & SLA	Case Assignment || System shall support automatic and manual assignment of cases to agents, departments, teams, supervisors, and support queues based on configurable business rules.
22.3.4	Marketing & CRM	Case Management & SLA	Case Workflow Management || System shall support configurable case statuses including Open, Assigned, In Progress, Pending Customer, Pending Third Party, Escalated, Resolved, Reopened, and Closed.
22.3.5	Marketing & CRM	Case Management & SLA	SLA Management || System shall support configurable service-level agreements including first response targets, resolution targets, escalation thresholds, business hours calendars, and SLA breach monitoring.
22.3.6	Marketing & CRM	Case Management & SLA	Case Escalation Management || System shall support automated and manual escalation workflows based on case priority, severity, SLA breaches, unresolved duration, business rules, and managerial approval.
22.3.7	Marketing & CRM	Case Management & SLA	Agent Notes & Attachments || Authorized users shall be able to add internal notes, public notes, attachments, documents, images, screenshots, voice recordings, and supporting evidence to service cases.
22.3.8	Marketing & CRM	Case Management & SLA	Omnichannel Communication Tracking || System shall maintain communication history related to cases including email, SMS, WhatsApp, live chat, phone calls, push notifications, CRM campaigns, and guest interactions.
22.3.9	Marketing & CRM	Case Management & SLA	Case Reporting & Analytics || System shall provide reporting and dashboards covering case volume, response times, resolution times, SLA compliance, complaint trends, escalation trends, agent productivity, and customer satisfaction metrics.
22.3.10	Marketing & CRM	Case Management & SLA	Case Audit Trail || System shall maintain a complete audit trail of case creation, assignments, ownership changes, status changes, escalations, communications, approvals, resolutions, and administrative actions.
22.3.1	Marketing & CRM	Case Management & SLA	Visual Journey Builder || System shall provide a drag-and-drop visual journey builder allowing marketing users to design automated customer journeys using events, conditions, actions, delays, approvals, and communication steps without requiring technical development.
22.3.2	Marketing & CRM	Case Management & SLA	Event Trigger Engine || System shall automatically trigger marketing journeys based on business events including ticket purchases, reservations, check-ins, membership renewals, membership expiration, loyalty transactions, wallet top-ups, refunds, cancellations, birthdays, anniversaries, survey responses, and guest inactivity.
22.3.3	Marketing & CRM	Case Management & SLA	Multi-Channel Automation || Automated journeys shall support email, SMS, WhatsApp, push notifications, mobile app notifications, web notifications, CRM tasks, call center actions, and third-party integrations.
22.3.4	Marketing & CRM	Case Management & SLA	Conditional Logic & Decision Trees || System shall support complex automation rules using IF/THEN conditions based on guest profile data, loyalty status, membership type, spend levels, visit history, survey responses, wallet balance, campaign interactions, and custom attributes.
22.3.5	Marketing & CRM	Case Management & SLA	Wait & Scheduling Controls || Marketing journeys shall support configurable delays, wait periods, business hours, time zones, blackout periods, expiration windows, and recurring schedules.
22.3.6	Marketing & CRM	Case Management & SLA	Abandoned Cart Recovery Automation || System shall automatically identify abandoned purchases and trigger recovery campaigns through multiple channels with configurable reminders, offers, incentives, and expiration rules.
22.3.7	Marketing & CRM	Case Management & SLA	Membership Lifecycle Automation || System shall automate membership acquisition, onboarding, benefit education, renewal reminders, upgrade offers, expiration warnings, and win-back campaigns.
22.3.8	Marketing & CRM	Case Management & SLA	Loyalty Lifecycle Automation || System shall automate loyalty enrollment, points earning notifications, reward reminders, tier upgrade communications, point expiration alerts, and retention campaigns.
22.3.9	Marketing & CRM	Case Management & SLA	Wallet Automation || System shall automate wallet-related communications including top-up confirmations, low balance alerts, wallet promotions, wallet inactivity reminders, and bonus credit campaigns.
22.3.10	Marketing & CRM	Case Management & SLA	Birthday & Anniversary Campaigns || System shall automatically generate personalized birthday, anniversary, membership anniversary, and milestone campaigns including offers, rewards, vouchers, and loyalty incentives.
22.3.11	Marketing & CRM	Case Management & SLA	Guest Onboarding Journeys || System shall support automated onboarding journeys for new guests, new members, loyalty enrollments, app installations, and first-time visitors.
22.3.12	Marketing & CRM	Case Management & SLA	Re-Engagement Campaigns || System shall automatically identify inactive guests and launch targeted win-back campaigns based on inactivity periods, purchase history, loyalty status, and visit behavior.
22.3.13	Marketing & CRM	Case Management & SLA	Survey & Feedback Automation || System shall automatically trigger surveys, NPS requests, reviews, and feedback collection based on visits, purchases, support interactions, or completed experiences.
22.3.14	Marketing & CRM	Case Management & SLA	Cross-Sell & Upsell Automation || System shall automatically recommend products, memberships, packages, upgrades, F&B offers, retail items, experiences, and loyalty rewards based on guest behavior and purchase history.
22.3.15	Marketing & CRM	Case Management & SLA	Reservation Follow-Up Automation || System shall automate reservation confirmations, reminders, pre-arrival communications, operational notifications, and post-visit follow-up activities.
22.3.16	Marketing & CRM	Case Management & SLA	Case & Service Recovery Automation || System shall automatically create tasks, escalation workflows, compensation offers, and recovery communications following complaints, low survey scores, or negative reviews.
22.3.17	Marketing & CRM	Case Management & SLA	AI Journey Recommendations || AI shall recommend marketing journeys, communication flows, audience targeting strategies, campaign timing, and automation opportunities based on historical performance.
22.3.18	Marketing & CRM	Case Management & SLA	AI Content Generation || AI shall generate email content, SMS messages, WhatsApp templates, push notifications, landing page content, promotional offers, and personalized messaging.
22.3.19	Marketing & CRM	Case Management & SLA	AI Send Time Optimization || AI shall determine the optimal communication time for each guest based on historical engagement patterns, location, language, behavior, and channel preferences.
22.3.20	Marketing & CRM	Case Management & SLA	AI Offer Optimization || AI shall recommend discounts, promotions, loyalty rewards, upgrades, bundles, and incentives that maximize conversion and revenue while respecting business rules.

22.4.13	Marketing & CRM	Newsletter & Email Marketing	A/B Testing || System shall support testing of subject lines, content layouts, offers, images, send times, and call-to-action variations with automated winner selection.
22.4.14	Marketing & CRM	Newsletter & Email Marketing	Deliverability Management || System shall monitor bounce rates, spam scores, unsubscribe rates, domain reputation, and email delivery health.

22.4.17	Marketing & CRM	Newsletter & Email Marketing	Revenue Attribution || System shall calculate revenue generated by each newsletter and campaign, including direct and assisted conversions.

22.5.2	Marketing & CRM	Reviews & Ratings	Multi-Channel Review Capture || Reviews may be collected through email, SMS, WhatsApp, mobile application, website, kiosks, surveys, QR codes, and third-party review platforms.

22.5.4	Marketing & CRM	Reviews & Ratings	Category-Based Reviews || Guests shall be able to rate specific areas such as attractions, events, facilities, staff, food and beverage, retail, cleanliness, accessibility, membership experience, mobile application, and overall satisfaction.
22.5.5	Marketing & CRM	Reviews & Ratings	Review Moderation Workflow || System shall support review approval, rejection, escalation, hiding, publishing, and moderation workflows with configurable user permissions and approval rules.
22.5.6	Marketing & CRM	Reviews & Ratings	Review Publishing || Approved reviews may be published automatically on websites, mobile applications, event pages, attraction pages, membership portals, and marketing channels.
22.5.7	Marketing & CRM	Reviews & Ratings	Review Responses || Authorized staff shall be able to respond to guest reviews, provide feedback, acknowledge issues, and communicate resolution updates.
22.5.8	Marketing & CRM	Reviews & Ratings	Review Escalation Rules || System shall automatically escalate reviews meeting predefined criteria such as low ratings, negative sentiment, complaints, safety concerns, refund requests, or operational incidents.
22.5.9	Marketing & CRM	Reviews & Ratings	AI Sentiment Analysis || AI shall analyze review content and classify sentiment as positive, neutral, negative, highly positive, or highly negative while identifying underlying themes and trends.
22.5.10	Marketing & CRM	Reviews & Ratings	AI Topic Detection || AI shall automatically identify recurring topics including pricing, staff service, wait times, cleanliness, ticketing experience, memberships, loyalty programs, food quality, events, facilities, and operational issues.
22.5.11	Marketing & CRM	Reviews & Ratings	AI Issue Detection || AI shall automatically identify potential operational issues, service failures, safety concerns, reputational risks, and urgent matters requiring immediate attention.
22.5.12	Marketing & CRM	Reviews & Ratings	AI Review Summarization || AI shall generate summarized insights from large volumes of reviews to assist management in identifying trends and improvement opportunities.
22.5.13	Marketing & CRM	Reviews & Ratings	Service Recovery Automation || Negative reviews may automatically trigger compensation workflows, follow-up communications, loyalty rewards, support cases, management alerts, or guest recovery campaigns.
22.5.14	Marketing & CRM	Reviews & Ratings	CRM Integration || Reviews and ratings shall automatically become part of the guest’s CRM profile and engagement history.
22.5.15	Marketing & CRM	Reviews & Ratings	Loyalty Integration || System shall support rewarding guests with loyalty points, badges, incentives, or promotional benefits for submitting reviews and feedback.
22.5.16	Marketing & CRM	Reviews & Ratings	Membership Integration || Review activity shall be linked to memberships and membership analytics to understand satisfaction across membership tiers and programs.
22.5.17	Marketing & CRM	Reviews & Ratings	Mobile App Review Center || Guests shall be able to view, submit, edit, and manage reviews directly through the guest mobile application.

22.5.19	Marketing & CRM	Reviews & Ratings	Benchmarking & Trend Analysis || System shall provide historical comparisons and trend analysis to identify improvements or declines in guest satisfaction over time.
22.5.20	Marketing & CRM	Reviews & Ratings	Review Audit Trail || System shall maintain a complete audit history of review creation, moderation, edits, responses, escalations, AI classifications, and administrative actions.

22.14.12	Marketing & CRM	Audience Segmentation	AI Lookalike Audiences || AI shall generate lookalike audiences based on characteristics of top-performing guest segments, members, loyal customers, or high-value visitors.

22.14.14	Marketing & CRM	Audience Segmentation	AI Upgrade Opportunities || AI shall identify guests likely to purchase memberships, loyalty upgrades, premium experiences, packages, annual passes, or upsell products.

22.14.19	Marketing & CRM	Audience Segmentation	Campaign Revenue Attribution || System shall identify which campaigns generated ticket sales, memberships, loyalty activity, wallet transactions, reservations, and other revenue streams.
22.14.20	Marketing & CRM	Audience Segmentation	Membership Attribution || System shall measure which campaigns, channels, and interactions influenced membership purchases, renewals, upgrades, and retention.
22.14.21	Marketing & CRM	Audience Segmentation	Loyalty Attribution || System shall track how loyalty campaigns, rewards, challenges, and engagement initiatives influence revenue and retention.
22.14.22	Marketing & CRM	Audience Segmentation	Wallet Attribution || System shall measure the impact of wallet promotions, top-up campaigns, and wallet engagement initiatives on guest spending behavior.
22.14.23	Marketing & CRM	Audience Segmentation	Marketing ROI Dashboard || System shall provide dashboards displaying campaign ROI, cost per acquisition, revenue attribution, conversion rates, customer acquisition cost, and lifetime value performance.

23.1.1	Digital Asset Management	Digital Asset Management	System shall provide a centralized repository for storing and managing digital assets including images, videos, documents, PDFs, marketing materials, brand assets, audio files, templates, and creative content.
23.1.2	Digital Asset Management	Digital Asset Management	System shall support configurable asset categories, folders, collections, tags, and classifications.
23.1.3	Digital Asset Management	Digital Asset Management	System shall support configurable metadata including asset type, owner, department, campaign, venue, event, creation date, expiry date, and usage rights.
23.1.4	Digital Asset Management	Digital Asset Management	Authorized users shall upload assets individually or in bulk through web interfaces and APIs.
23.1.5	Digital Asset Management	Digital Asset Management	System shall support searching assets using keywords, metadata, tags, categories, and filters.
23.1.6	Digital Asset Management	Digital Asset Management	AI shall support semantic search allowing users to locate assets using natural language queries.
23.1.7	Digital Asset Management	Digital Asset Management	AI shall automatically generate tags, keywords, and classifications for uploaded assets.
23.1.8	Digital Asset Management	Digital Asset Management	System shall maintain historical versions of assets and allow comparison, rollback, and restoration.
23.1.9	Digital Asset Management	Digital Asset Management	System shall support configurable approval workflows for asset publication and usage.
23.1.10	Digital Asset Management	Digital Asset Management	System shall support secure sharing of assets across departments, venues, tenants, and external partners.
23.1.11	Digital Asset Management	Digital Asset Management	System shall track asset ownership, copyright information, licensing terms, and expiration dates.
23.1.12	Digital Asset Management	Digital Asset Management	System shall track where assets are used across websites, mobile applications, campaigns, kiosks, emails, and digital channels.
23.1.13	Digital Asset Management	Digital Asset Management	System shall distribute approved assets to websites, mobile apps, kiosks, CRM campaigns, and digital channels.
23.1.14	Digital Asset Management	Digital Asset Management	System shall provide analytics on asset usage, downloads, views, distribution, and performance.
23.1.15	Digital Asset Management	Digital Asset Management	System shall expose DAM functionality through APIs and support integration with CMS, CRM, marketing platforms, mobile applications, and third-party systems.
